```python
from app.engine.pipeline import run_pipeline, load_rules, save_rules
state = run_pipeline(rules=load_rules())   # pure, ~1s, deterministic; cache it in memory, re-run on POST /run or after rules change
state = {
  "as_of": "2026-10-31",
  "metrics": {"overdue_balance": float, "overdue_count": int, "open_ar": float,
              "cash_found": float,            # $ of unapplied receipts matched to invoices that looked unpaid
              "chases_prevented": int,        # reminders that would have gone to customers who already paid / are in dispute
              "auto_applied": int, "needs_review": int, "held": int, "drafts": int, "human_writes": int,
              "collections_complaints": int},  # the veto metric
  "payments": [ {"payment_id","received_date","amount","method","payer_name","memo","customer_id","customer_name",
                 "decision": "AUTO_APPLY"|"PROPOSE"|"EXCEPTION", "invoice_ids": [str], "matched_amount": float,
                 "difference": float, "confidence": float, "reasons": [str], "agent": "Cash Application"} ],
  "invoices": [ {"invoice_id","customer_id","customer_name","amount","issue_date","due_date","days_past_due","ar_status",
                 "stage": "courtesy"|"first"|"second"|"final"|"escalate"|"card_failed"|"not_due",
                 "action": "DRAFT_REMINDER"|"HOLD"|"HUMAN_WRITES"|"NO_ACTION",
                 "holds": [{"code": str, "label": str, "detail": str, "agent": str}],
                 "draft": None or {"to": [str], "cc": [str], "subject": str, "body": str, "template": str, "source": "claude"|"template"},
                 "top50": bool, "account_manager": str, "reasons": [str]} ],
  "customers": { customer_id: {"customer_id","name","plan","current_arr","top50","rank","account_manager","renewal_date",
                 "payment_method","open_tickets":[{ticket_id,created_at,subject,category,priority,status,summary}],
                 "recent_tickets":[...], "billing_complaint": bool, "nps_last": int or None, "usage_trend": str,
                 "contacts":[{name,email,role,status,verified: bool,issue: str or None}], "open_invoices":[invoice_id],
                 "unapplied_payments":[payment_id], "flags":[str]} },
  "audit": [ {"ts","agent","object_type","object_id","decision","confidence","reason"} ],
  "rules": {...}  # same dict as load_rules()
}
rules = load_rules()  # dict, persisted JSON. Keys include: auto_apply_exact_ref_match (bool), writeoff_threshold (float, default 0),
                      # amount_tolerance (float), reminder_days {"courtesy":-5,"first":7,"second":21,"final":45,"escalate":90},
                      # top50_human_only (bool), hold_on_open_ticket_days (int), banned_phrases ([str]), notify_am_before_contact (bool)
save_rules(dict)
```

BUILD (FastAPI + Jinja2 + a little HTMX from a pinned CDN, e.g. htmx.org@1.9.12 on unpkg; plain CSS, clean, professional, finance-y, light theme; readable at laptop width; no SPA):
- `app/main.py` — app factory, routes, mounts static. Run: `uvicorn app.main:app`.
- `app/auth.py` — Google OAuth (Authlib). Env: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET, SESSION_SECRET, ALLOWED_DOMAINS (default "scaleworks.com"), ALLOWED_EMAILS (comma list, for Jacek's own accounts), BASE_URL. Check the `hd` claim AND the email domain AND email_verified. If GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID is unset → dev mode: auto-login as "dev@localhost" with a visible yellow "DEV MODE — auth off" banner. /login, /auth/callback, /logout. Every page except /login, /healthz requires auth.
- `app/store.py` — SQLite (path from env DB_PATH, default ./ledgerline.db): table `actions` (id, ts, user_email, object_type, object_id, action [approve|reject|edit|override_hold|release_hold|send|apply], note, payload_json). Approving a draft = "approve & mark sent (simulated — no email leaves this demo)". Applying a payment proposal = "approve application (simulated ledger write)". Overriding a hold requires a typed reason. Human actions appear in the audit trail alongside agent decisions.
- Pages (top nav: Today · Cash application · Collections · Customers · Audit trail · Rules · About):
  1. `/` Today (Priya's morning view): metric cards (overdue balance BIG; cash found; chases prevented; drafts awaiting approval; holds; collections complaints = "veto" card), then "Needs you today" list ordered by $ with one-line reason, then a strip explaining the 4 agents (Cash Application → Account Context → Controller → Collections) with counts.
  2. `/cash` payments table: decision badge, matched invoices, difference, confidence, reasons (expand), Approve/Reject buttons for PROPOSE; AUTO_APPLY rows show "applied by agent (exact amount + invoice reference)" with an Undo.
  3. `/collections` invoices grouped by action (HOLD, HUMAN_WRITES, DRAFT_REMINDER, NO_ACTION). Each row expands (HTMX or <details>) to show holds with agent + plain-English detail, and the draft email (editable textarea → Approve & send (simulated) / Reject). Override-hold button with reason.
  4. `/customers/{id}` 360 view from state["customers"][id]: header (plan, ARR, rank, AM, renewal), open invoices, unapplied payments, tickets, contacts with verified/issue badges, flags. `/customers` searchable list of customers with open AR.
  5. `/audit` merged chronological list: agent decisions (state["audit"]) + human actions (store), filterable by agent; CSV download.
  6. `/rules` form editing load_rules() keys (Priya can change thresholds herself — Maya said that's what decides whether it survives); saving calls save_rules, re-runs the pipeline, logs an action, shows "what changed" (counts before/after).
  7. `/about` renders `submission/ONE_PAGER.md` (markdown → HTML; use the `markdown` package — pip install it into the venv and add to requirements) and, if env DEMO_VIDEO_URL is set, embeds the video (Loom/YouTube/Drive embed URL) above it. If the file is missing, show "coming soon".
  8. `/healthz` → ok.
- `requirements.txt` (pinned versions matching the venv: run pip freeze for the packages used), `render.yaml` (one web service, python runtime, PYTHON_VERSION 3.11.9, build `pip install -r requirements.txt`, start `uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port $PORT`, healthCheckPath /healthz, env vars listed with sync:false for secrets, ALLOWED_DOMAINS=scaleworks.com, a 1GB disk mounted at /var/data with DB_PATH=/var/data/ledgerline.db and RULES_PATH=/var/data/rules.json — note in README the disk needs a paid instance; if on free plan, state is ephemeral and that's acceptable for a demo).
- `README.md` — what it is (2 paragraphs), run locally in 3 commands, env vars table, deploy to Render, how Google OAuth client is set up (Authorized redirect URI = <BASE_URL>/auth/callback), and a "Simulated vs real" section (no emails are sent, no ledger writes).
- `.gitignore` (.venv, *.db, .env, __pycache__, rules.json runtime copy if outside app/).

Verify: start uvicorn locally on port 8765 in dev mode, curl every page for 200, then stop it. Report back ≤12 lines: files created, how you verified, anything I must know to integrate. Also give me one line for trail/JOURNAL.md.

```
# Ledgerline AI Operator Challenge — project instructions

Read `docs/PLAN.md` and `docs/BRIEF.md` first. This is a 24-hour Scaleworks vetting test. Jacek (CEO of a
portfolio company) is the operator. The deliverable is ONE finance process working end to end on real records,
run by a team of scoped agents (SpaceXSI Grok-bot pattern).

## Team (Claude Code subagents in .claude/agents/)
- **test-steward**: owns the challenge itself: the paper trail, the Maya Q&A log, the submission checklist, the one-pager,
  the video script and the transcript export. Invoke it at every milestone.
- **data-analyst**: profiles the data room, produces evidence numbers, never edits data.
- **builder**: writes the product (FastAPI + Claude API agents + approval UI + audit log, deployed to Render with Google SSO).
- **qa-evaluator**: measures accuracy, builds the "where it gets things wrong" gallery, tries to break the guardrails.

Run independent work in parallel subagents. Keep the main thread for decisions.

## Paper-trail rules (non-negotiable)
1. After every meaningful step, append one line to `trail/JOURNAL.md`: `| <local time> | T+hh:mm | <who> | <what> | <why> |`.
   Keep it high level and in plain English.
2. Every real decision (what we chose, what we rejected, and why) goes in `trail/DECISIONS.md`.
3. Every question to Maya and her answer goes **word for word** in `trail/MAYA_QA.md`.
4. Dead ends are logged too. The brief says the reviewers want them.
5. The timer start time is recorded in `trail/JOURNAL.md` the moment Jacek presses "Begin day one". All T+ times count from it.

## Maya loop (standing instruction from Jacek)
Keep Maya (COO) in the loop throughout the day. At each milestone the test-steward drafts one message in
`trail/MAYA_OUTBOX.md`. The main thread sends it via the Maya chat at apply.scaleworks.com/build (Jacek's Chrome),
pastes the reply back, and the steward logs it word for word and records what changed. Budget: 60 messages, aim for ≤ 12.

## Product guardrails (judgment is graded)
- Deterministic code does arithmetic, joins and exact matching. The LLM does fuzzy matching, text reading, tone and explanations.
- No customer-facing send, credit, write-off or ledger change without a human approval in the UI.
- Every agent action → an audit-log row (agent, input refs, decision, confidence, reason, approver).
- If a feature needs data the room doesn't have, generate it, label it SYNTHETIC, and say so.

## Do not
- Touch the Scaleworks site's "Begin day
… [result truncated, 13472 chars total]
```

```
# Collections policy

- Invoices are due 30 days from issue. Card customers are charged on the invoice date.
- Reminders: a courtesy note 5 days before the due date, then at 7, 21 and 45 days past due.
- No message may threaten suspension or legal action without the VP Finance's sign-off. The CEO reads complaints about collections personally.
- Nothing goes to one of the 50 largest accounts without a person reading it first.
- Do not chase an invoice the customer has disputed, or a customer with an open support escalation, until the account manager says so. Tell the account manager before finance contacts one of their accounts.
- Payment plans: the VP Finance can approve up to 90 days. Longer needs the CFO role, currently held by the CEO.
- Write-offs: the VP Finance can approve up to $2,000. Card invoices unpaid after 150 days are written off automatically.
- Cash application: bank transfers and cheques are matched to invoices by hand and can take three to five working days to show as paid.

 "file_path": "/Users/jacekmaterna/Library/Application Support/Claude/scratch-workspaces/e69441d8-02c7-4ae1-82fa-e65f61df0516/362a2cee-265f-40fd-99ca-8b843a81ad27/scratch-2026-11-20-a3ea2a/app/web/state.py",
 "content": "\"\"\"In-memory cache of the pipeline state.\n\nUses the real engine (app.engine.pipeline) when it imports, otherwise the fake in app/web/fake_state.py.\nThe import is retried on every refresh, so once the engine lands a POST /run picks it up without a restart.\n\"\"\"\nimport importlib\nimport threading\nimport time\nimport traceback\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Optional\n\n_lock = threading.Lock()\n_cache: Dict[str, Any] = {\"state\": None, \"source\": None, \"error\": None, \"ran_at\": None, \"seconds\": None}\n\n\ndef _backend():\n    \"\"\"Return (module, source, error).\"\"\"\n    try:\n        mod = importlib.import_module(\"app.engine.pipeline\")\n        for name in (\"run_pipeline\", \"load_rules\", \"save_rules\"):\n            if not hasattr(mod, name):\n                raise ImportError(\"app.engine.pipeline has no \" + name)\n        return mod, \"engine\", None\n    except Exception as exc:  # engine missing or broken: fall back, but say so loudly in the UI\n        from app.web import fake_state\n        return fake_state, \"fake\", \"{}: {}\".format(type(exc).__name__, exc)\n\n\ndef load_rules() -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    mod, _, _ = _backend()\n    return mod.load_rules()\n\n\ndef save_rules(rules: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    mod, _, _ = _backend()\n    mod.save_rules(rules)\n\n\ndef refresh() -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    with _lock:\n        mod, source, err = _backend()\n        t0 = time.time()\n        try:\n            state = mod.run_pipeline(rules=mod.load_rules())\n        except Exception as exc:\n            if source == \"engine\":\n                from app.web import fake_state\n                err = \"Engine run failed, showing fake data: {}: {}\\n{}\".format(\n                    type(exc).__name__, exc, traceback.format_exc(limit=3))\n                source = \"fake\"\n                state = fake_state.run_pipeline(rules=fake_state.load_rules())\n            else:\n                raise\n        _cache.update(state=state, source=source, error=err, ran_at=time.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\"),\n                      seconds=round(time.time() - t0, 2))\n        return state\n\n\ndef get_state() -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    if _cache[\"state\"] is None:\n        return refresh()\n    return _cache[\"state\"]\n\n\ndef info() -> Dict[str, Optional[Any]]:\n    return {k: _cache[k] for k in (\"source\", \"error\", \"ran_at\", \"seconds\")}\n"
}

{
 "file_path": "/Users/jacekmaterna/Library/Application Support/Claude/scratch-workspaces/e69441d8-02c7-4ae1-82fa-e65f61df0516/362a2cee-265f-40fd-99ca-8b843a81ad27/scratch-2026-11-20-a3ea2a/app/store.py",
 "content": "\"\"\"Append-only store of human actions (approve, reject, edit, override_hold, release_hold, send, apply).\n\nNothing here sends email or writes to a ledger: every action is a simulated record that shows up in the audit trail.\nRows are never updated or deleted; the latest row for an object is its current human status.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nimport threading\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\n\nACTIONS = (\"approve\", \"reject\", \"edit\", \"override_hold\", \"release_hold\", \"send\", \"apply\")\n\n_lock = threading.Lock()\n\n\ndef db_path() -> str:\n    return os.environ.get(\"DB_PATH\", os.path.join(os.getcwd(), \"ledgerline.db\"))\n\n\ndef _connect() -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    path = db_path()\n    d = os.path.dirname(path)\n    if d:\n        os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db() -> None:\n    with _lock, _connect() as conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS actions (\n                   id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                   ts TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   user_email TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   object_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   object_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   action TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   note TEXT,\n                   payload_json TEXT\n               )\"\"\")\n        conn.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_actions_obj ON actions(object_type, object_id)\")\n\n\ndef record(user_email: str, object_type: str, object_id: str, action: str, note: str = \"\",\n           payload: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> int:\n    if action not in ACTIONS:\n        raise ValueError(\"unknown action \" + action)\n    ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\")\n    with _lock, _connect() as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO actions (ts, user_email, object_type, object_id, action, note, payload_json) \"\n            \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (ts, user_email, object_type, object_id, action, note or \"\", json.dumps(payload or {}, default=str)))\n        return int(cur.lastrowid)\n\n\ndef _row(r: sqlite3.Row) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    d = dict(r)\n    try:\n        d[\"payload\"] = json.loads(d.get(\"payload_json\") or \"{}\")\n    except ValueError:\n        d[\"payload\"] = {}\n    return d\n\n\ndef all_actions() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    with _connect() as conn:\n        return [_row(r) for r in conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM actions ORDER BY id\")]\n\n\ndef latest_by_object() -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Map 'object_type:object_id' -> latest action row.\"\"\"\n    out: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n    for a in all_actions():\n        out[a[\"object_type\"] + \":\" + a[\"object_id\"]] = a\n    return out\n\n\ndef history(object_type: str, object_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    with _connect() as conn:\n        return [_row(r) for r in conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT * FROM actions WHERE object_type = ? AND object_id = ? ORDER BY id\", (object_type, object_id))]\n"
}
```

{
 "file_path": "/Users/jacekmaterna/Library/Application Support/Claude/scratch-workspaces/e69441d8-02c7-4ae1-82fa-e65f61df0516/362a2cee-265f-40fd-99ca-8b843a81ad27/scratch-2026-11-20-a3ea2a/app/auth.py",
 "content": "\"\"\"Google OAuth (Authlib) with a domain + email allow-list.\n\nEnv:\n  GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID / GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET  OAuth client. If GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID is unset the app runs in DEV MODE:\n                                           everyone is auto-logged-in as dev@localhost and a yellow banner says so.\n  SESSION_SECRET                           signs the session cookie.\n  ALLOWED_DOMAINS                          comma list, default \"scaleworks.com\". Needs hd claim == domain AND email\n                                           domain == domain AND email_verified.\n  ALLOWED_EMAILS                           comma list of individual addresses always allowed (email_verified needed).\n  BASE_URL                                 public URL, used to build the redirect URI <BASE_URL>/auth/callback.\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport secrets\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse\n\nDEV_EMAIL = \"dev@localhost\"\nPUBLIC_PATHS = (\"/login\", \"/auth/callback\", \"/logout\", \"/healthz\")\nPUBLIC_PREFIXES = (\"/static/\",)\n\n\ndef dev_mode() -> bool:\n    return not os.environ.get(\"GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID\")\n\n\ndef _csv(name: str, default: str = \"\") -> List[str]:\n    return [x.strip().lower() for x in os.environ.get(name, default).split(\",\") if x.strip()]\n\n\ndef allowed_domains() -> List[str]:\n    return _csv(\"ALLOWED_DOMAINS\", \"scaleworks.com\")\n\n\ndef allowed_emails() -> List[str]:\n    return _csv(\"ALLOWED_EMAILS\")\n\n\ndef session_secret() -> str:\n    s = os.environ.get(\"SESSION_SECRET\")\n    if s:\n        return s\n    # Fine for dev; in production set SESSION_SECRET or sessions reset on every restart.\n    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef is_allowed(userinfo: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:\n    email = (userinfo.get(\"email\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n    if not email:\n        return False, \"Google did not return an email address.\"\n    verified = userinfo.get(\"email_verified\")\n    if verified not in (True, \"true\", \"True\", 1):\n        return False, \"Your Google email address is not verified.\"\n    if email in allowed_emails():\n        return True, \"allow-listed email\"\n    domain = email.rsplit(\"@\", 1)[-1]\n    hd = (userinfo.get(\"hd\") or \"\").strip().lower()\n    if domain in allowed_domains() and hd == domain:\n        return True, \"allowed Google Workspace domain\"\n    return False, \"{} is not allowed. Sign in with a {} Google Workspace account.\".format(\n        email, \" or \".join(allowed_domains()) or \"permitted\")\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request) -> Optional[str]:\n    if dev_mode():\n        return DEV_EMAIL\n    user = request.session.get(\"user\")\n    return user.get(\"email\") if user else None\n\n\ndef is_public(path: str) -> bool:\n    return path in PUBLIC_PATHS or any(path.startswith(p) for p in PUBLIC_PREFIXES)\n\n\n_oauth = None\n\n\ndef _get_oauth():\n    global _oauth\n    if _oauth is None:\n        from authlib.integrations.starlette_client import OAuth\n        _oauth = OAuth()\n        _oauth.register(\n            name=\"google\",\n            client_id=os.environ.get(\"GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID\"),\n            client_secret=os.environ.get(\"GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET\"),\n            server_metadata_url=\"https://accounts.google.com/.well-known/openid-configuration\",\n            client_kwargs={\"scope\": \"openid email profile\"},\n        )\n    return _oauth\n\n\ndef _redirect_uri(request: Request) -> str:\n    base = os.environ.get(\"BASE_URL\", \"\").rstrip(\"/\")\n    if base:\n        return base + \"/auth/callback\"\n    return str(request.url_for(\"auth_callback\"))\n\n\ndef build_router(templates) -> APIRouter:\n    router = APIRouter()\n\n    @router.get(\"/login\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\n    async def login(request: Request, error: str = \"\"):\n        if dev_mode():\n            return RedirectResponse(\"/\", status_code=303)\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(request, \"login.html\", {\n            \"error\": error, \"domains\": allowed_domains(), \"user\": None, \"dev_mode\": False})\n\n    @router.get(\"/login/google\")\n    async def login_google(request: Request):\n        if dev_mode():\n            return RedirectResponse(\"/\", status_code=303)\n        return await _get_oauth().google.authorize_redirect(request, _redirect_uri(request),\n                                                            prompt=\"select_account\")\n\n    @router.get(\"/auth/callback\", name=\"auth_callback\")\n    async def auth_callback(request: Request):\n        if dev_mode():\n            return RedirectResponse(\"/\", status_code=303)\n        try:\n            token = await _get_oauth().google.authorize_access_token(request)\n        except Exception as exc:  # state mismatch, denied consent, etc.\n            return templates.TemplateResponse(request, \"login.html\", {\n                \"error\": \"Sign-in failed: {}\".format(exc), \"domains\": allowed_domains(), \"user\": None,\n                \"dev_mode\": False}, status_code=400)\n        userinfo = dict(token.get(\"userinfo\") or {})\n        ok, why = is_allowed(userinfo)\n        if not ok:\n            request.session.pop(\"user\", None)\n            return templates.TemplateResponse(request, \"login.html\", {\n                \"error\": why, \"domains\": allowed_domains(), \"user\": None, \"dev_mode\": False}, status_code=403)\n        request.session[\"user\"] = {\"email\": userinfo[\"email\"].lower(), \"name\": userinfo.get(\"name\", \"\")}\n        nxt = request.session.pop(\"next\", \"/\")\n        if not isinstance(nxt, str) or not nxt.startswith(\"/\") or nxt.startswith(\"//\"):\n            nxt = \"/\"\n

 "content": "\"\"\"Ledgerline Order-to-Cash operator: FastAPI app factory.\n\nRun locally:  uvicorn app.main:app --reload\n\"\"\"\nimport os\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, store\nfrom app.web.views import STATIC_DIR, build_router, templates\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"Ledgerline AR Operator\", docs_url=None, redoc_url=None, openapi_url=None)\n    store.init_db()\n\n    @app.middleware(\"http\")\n    async def require_login(request: Request, call_next):\n        path = request.url.path\n        if auth.is_public(path) or auth.current_user(request):\n            return await call_next(request)\n        if request.headers.get(\"HX-Request\"):\n            return PlainTextResponse(\"login required\", status_code=401, headers={\"HX-Redirect\": \"/login\"})\n        if request.method == \"GET\":\n            nxt = path + ((\"?\" + request.url.query) if request.url.query else \"\")\n            request.session[\"next\"] = nxt\n            return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n        return JSONResponse({\"detail\": \"login required\"}, status_code=401)\n\n    # ### Claude · 2026-11-20 17:27:10 UTC
**Tool call:** `Write`
```json
{
 "file_path": "/Users/jacekmaterna/Library/Application Support/Claude/scratch-workspaces/e69441d8-02c7-4ae1-82fa-e65f61df0516/362a2cee-265f-40fd-99ca-8b843a81ad27/scratch-2026-11-20-a3ea2a/README.md",
 "content": "# Ledgerline AR Operator\n\nAn AI order-to-cash team for Ledgerline's finance function. Four scoped agents run in a fixed order on every cycle:\n**Cash Application** matches unapplied bank receipts to open invoices, **Account Context** builds a 360 of each\ncustomer (tickets, NPS, usage, contract, renewal, contacts), the **Controller** holds anything that must not go out\n(money already received, an open complaint, a billing-rewrite dispute, a renewal in flight, an unverified contact,\nthreatening wording), and **Collections** ranks overdue AR, picks the stage and tone under the collections policy and\ndrafts the reminder. Cash Application runs first, so a receipt that plausibly pays an invoice blocks any chase on it.\n\nIt is built for Priya, the AR specialist who runs it every morning. Every decision carries a confidence and a\nplain-English reason, every human action is logged next to the agent decisions in an append-only audit trail, and\nPriya can change the thresholds herself on the Rules page. Agents propose; a person approves anything that touches a\ncustomer or the ledger. The only thing an agent does alone is apply a receipt whose amount and invoice reference both\nmatch exactly, and Priya can undo that.\n\n## Run locally (3 commands)\n\n```bash\npython3 -m venv .venv && . .venv/bin/activate\npip install -r requirements.txt\nuvicorn app.main:app --reload        # open http://127.0.0.1:8000\n```\n\nWith no `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` set the app runs in **dev mode**: you are signed in as `dev@localhost` and a yellow\n\"DEV MODE: auth off\" banner is shown on every page. Runs on Python 3.9+ (Render uses 3.11.9).\n\n## Pages\n\n| Page | What it is for |\n|---|---|\n| Today `/` | Overdue balance, cash found, chases prevented, drafts awaiting approval, holds, and the collections-complaints veto counter. \"Needs you today\" list, largest first. |\n| Cash application `/cash` | Receipts with the agent's decision (auto-applied / proposed / exception), matched invoices, difference, confidence and reasons. Approve or reject proposals; undo an auto-application. |\n| Collections `/collections` | Open invoices grouped by Held / Person writes / Draft / No action. Expand a row for holds, reasons and the editable draft. Approve & send (simulated), reject, or release/override a hold with a typed reason. |\n| Customers `/customers`, `/customers/{id}` | Searchable list of customers with open AR and the per-customer 360. |\n| Audit trail `/audit` | Agent decisions and human actions merged, filterable by agent, CSV download. |\n| Rules `/rules` | Edit thresholds (auto-apply, write-off threshold, tolerances, reminder schedule, top-50 rule, banned phrases...). Saving re-runs the agents and shows what changed. |\n| About `/about` | The one-pager (`submission/ONE_PAGER.md`) and the demo video if `DEMO_VIDEO_URL` is set. |\n| `/healthz` | Health check, returns `ok`. |\n\n## Environment variables\n\n| Variable | Required | Default | Purpose |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` | prod | unset = dev mode | Google OAuth client id. |\n| `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` | prod | | Google OAuth client secret. |\n| `SESSION_SECRET` | prod | random per process | Signs the session cookie. Render generates one. |\n| `BASE_URL` | prod | derived from request | Public URL, e.g. `https://ledgerline-ar-operator.onrender.com`. Redirect URI is `<BASE_URL>/auth/callback`. |\n| `ALLOWED_DOMAINS` | | `scaleworks.com` | Comma list. Sign-in needs Google `hd` claim = domain, email domain = domain, and a verified email. |\n| `ALLOWED_EMAILS` | | | Comma list of individual addresses always allowed (still need a verified email). |\n| `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` | optional | | Enables Claude for fuzzy matching and draft text. Without it the engine uses deterministic templates. |\n| `DB_PATH` | | `./ledgerline.db` | SQLite file for human actions. |\n| `RULES_PATH` | | `./rules.json` | JSON file holding the rules Priya edits. |\n| `DATA_DIR` | | `./data` | Data-room CSVs. |\n| `DEMO_VIDEO_URL` | | | Loom, YouTube or Google Drive link; embedded on `/about`. |\n\n## Deploy to Render\n\n1. Push the repo to GitHub (the `data/` folder must be included; the engine reads it at start-up).\n2. Render dashboard → **New → Blueprint** → pick the repo. `render.yaml` creates one Python web service\n   (Python 3.11.9, `pip install -r requirements.txt`, `uvicorn app.main:app`, health check `/healthz`).\n3. Fill in the `sync: false` variables: `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID`, `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET`, `BASE_URL`, `ALLOWED_EMAILS`,\n   `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, optionally `DEMO_VIDEO_URL`.\n4. The blueprint mounts a 1 GB disk at `/var/data` (`DB_PATH=/var/data/ledgerline.db`,\n   `RULES_PATH=/var/data/rules.json`). **Persistent disks need a paid instance.** On the free plan, delete the\n   `disk:` block: the app still works, but human actions and rule edits reset on each deploy or restart. That is\n   acceptable for a demo.\n\n## Google OAuth client\n\n1. Google Cloud Console → APIs & Services → **OAuth consent screen**: choose *External* (so allow-listed personal\n   accounts can sign in) or *Internal* if every user is in one Workspace. Scopes: `openid`, `email`, `profile`.\n2. **Credentials → Create credentials → OAuth client ID → Web application.**\n3. Authorized JavaScript origin: `<BASE_URL>`. Authorized redirect URI: **`<BASE_URL>/auth/callback`**\n   (add `http://localhost:8000/auth/callback` too if you want to test real sign-in locally).\n4. Put the client id and secret in Render's environment, never in the repo.\n\n## Simulated vs real\n\n| Real | Simulated |\n|---|---|\n| The data-room records (invoices, payments, AR aging, customers, contacts, contracts, tickets, NPS, usage, policy). | **No email is ever sent.** \"Approve & send\" records the approved text and marks it

```
def ai_on(state) -> bool:
    return bool((state.get("rules") or {}).get("use_claude"))


EMOJI = {":white_check_mark:": "\u2705", ":eyes:": "\U0001F440", ":rotating_light:": "\U0001F6A8",
         ":email:": "\U0001F4E7", ":credit_card:": "\U0001F4B3", ":raised_hand:": "\u270B", ":hand:": "\u270B",
         ":writing_hand:": "\u270D\uFE0F", ":warning:": "\u26A0\uFE0F", ":moneybag:": "\U0001F4B0"}


def slack_to_html(text: str) -> str:
    """Render Slack mrkdwn (*bold*, _italic_, <url|text>, <url>, :emoji:) to safe HTML."""
    from markupsafe import escape
    parts = []
    pos = 0
    for m in re.finditer(r"<([^<>|\s]+)(?:\|([^<>]+))?>", text):
        parts.append(str(escape(text[pos:m.start()])))
        url, label = m.group(1), m.group(2) or m.group(1)
        if re.match(r"^(https?://|/)", url):
            parts.append('<a href="{}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">{}</a>'.format(escape(url), escape(label)))
        else:
            parts.append(str(escape(m.group(0))))
        pos = m.end()
    parts.append(str(escape(text[pos:])))
    html = "".join(parts)
    html = re.sub(r"(?<![\w*])\*([^*\n]+)\*(?![\w*])", r"<b>\1</b>", html)
    html = re.sub(r"(?<![\w_])_([^_\n]+)_(?![\w_])", r"<i>\1</i>", html)
    for k, v in EMOJI.items():
        html = html.replace(k, v)
    return html.replace("\n", "<br>\n")


def _safe_back(request: Request, default: str = "/") -> str:
    ref = request.headers.get("referer") or ""
    m = re.match(r"^https?://[^/]+(/[^\s]*)$", ref)
    path = m.group(1) if m else default
    return path if path.startswith("/") and not path.startswith("//") else default
```

{
 "message": "All 6 additions are in, and every page plus /static returned 200 against the restarted engine on uvicorn port 8765 in dev mode (POST /run returned 303); the server is stopped.\n1. Card re-runs: /collections has a \"Card re-runs to approve\" group (10 invoices). Each row shows the evidence and its own Approve re-run (simulated charge) / Reject buttons, with no bulk button. The click is stored as `approve` or `reject` with payload `kind: card_retry`; tested and working.\n2. Holds show \"Routed to: X\" and what release requires. Releasing a DISPUTE or CANCELLATION hold returns 400 without the \"Account manager confirmed the dispute is settled\" box ticked and the AM's name filled in (prefilled from `route_to`). Both go in the payload. Override still works without them.\n3. Today: the veto card is green at 0 with your baseline sub-line (1 read by agent, 14 by keyword count). """Thin Claude wrapper with a disk cache so the demo is fast and repeatable.

If ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is not set (or use_claude is off), every call returns None and callers fall back to
deterministic logic. Cached answers are committed to the repo, so the deployed app shows Claude's output
without needing to call the API on each page load.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import os
import threading

CACHE_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "llm_cache.json")
MODEL = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_MODEL", "claude-sonnet-5-5")
_lock = threading.Lock()
_cache = None


def _load_cache():
    global _cache
    if _cache is None:
        try:
            with open(CACHE_PATH, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                _cache = json.load(f)
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            _cache = {}
    return _cache


def _save_cache():
    with open(CACHE_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:

```

def manual(name):
    p = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "manuals", name + ".md")
    try:
        with open(p, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return f.read()
    except OSError:
        return ""


def ask_json(task, system, user, enabled=True, max_tokens=900):
    """Return parsed JSON from Claude, or None. Results are cached by (task, system, user)."""
    key = hashlib.sha256((task + "\x00" + system + "\x00" + user).encode()).hexdigest()[:24]
    with _lock:
        cache = _load_cache()
        if key in cache:
            return cache[key]
    provider = os.environ.get("LLM_PROVIDER", "anthropic")  # "bedrock" keeps data inside Ledgerline's AWS account
    if not enabled or (provider == "anthropic" and not os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")):
        return None
    try:
        import anthropic

        client = anthropic.AnthropicBedrock() if provider == "bedrock" else anthropic.Anthropic()
        msg = client.messages.create(
            model=MODEL,
            max_tokens=max_tokens,
            system=system + "\n\nRespond with a single JSON object only. No prose, no code fences.",
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": user}],
        )
        text = "".join(b.text for b in msg.content if getattr(b, "type", "") == "text").strip()
        if text.startswith("```"):
            text = text.strip("`")
            text = text[text.find("{"):]
        data = json.loads(text[text.find("{"): text.rfind("}") + 1])
    except Exception as e:  # network, auth, parse: fall back, never break the pipeline
        print("[llm] %s failed: %s" % (task, e))
        return None
    with _lock:
        _cache[key] = data
        _save_cache()
    return data
def norm_name(s):
    """Normalise a company / payer name for matching: lower-case, & and + become 'and', punctuation removed."""
    if not isinstance(s, str):
        return ""
    s = s.lower().replace("&", " and ").replace("+", " and ")
    s = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s)
    return " ".join(s.split())


def core_tokens(s):
    return [t for t in norm_name(s).split() if t not in _SUFFIXES]

s=open(p).read()\nold='''        conn.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_actions_obj ON actions(object_type, object_id)\")\n'''\nnew='''        conn.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_actions_obj ON actions(object_type, object_id)\")\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS instructions (\n                   id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                   ts TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   user TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   agent TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   text TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'proposed',\n                   decided_by TEXT,\n                   decided_ts TEXT,\n                   source TEXT\n               )\"\"\")\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat (\n                   id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                   ts TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   user TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   agent TEXT,\n                   message TEXT NOT NULL,\n                   reply TEXT,\n                   mode TEXT\n               )\"\"\")\n'''\nassert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)\ns += '''\n\ndef _now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\")\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ instructions (humans -> agents)\nINSTRUCTION_STATUSES = (\"proposed\", \"accepted\", \"rejected\")\n\n\ndef add_instruction(user: str, agent: str, text: str, source: str = \"form\") -> int:\n    with _lock, _connect() as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO instructions (ts, user, agent, text, status, source) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                           (_now(), user, agent, text.strip(), \"proposed\", source))\n        return int(cur.lastrowid)\n\n\ndef instructions(agent: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    with _connect() as conn:\n        if agent:\n            rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM instructions WHERE agent = ? ORDER BY id DESC\", (agent,))\n        else:\n            rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM instructions ORDER BY id DESC\")\n        return [dict(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef get_instruction(iid: int) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    with _connect() as conn:\n        r = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM instructions WHERE id = ?\", (iid,)).fetchone()\n        return dict(r) if r else None\n\n\ndef decide_instruction(iid: int, status: str, user: str) -> None:\n    if status not in INSTRUCTION_STATUSES:\n        raise ValueError(status)\n    with _lock, _connect() as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE instructions SET status = ?, decided_by = ?, decided_ts = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, user, _now(), iid))\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ chat turns\ndef add_chat(user: str, agent: str, message: str, reply: str, mode: str) -> int:\n    with _lock, _connect() as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO chat (ts, user, agent, message, reply, mode) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                           (_now(), user, agent, message, reply, mode))\n        return int(cur.lastrowid)\n\n\ndef chats(limit: int = 500) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    with _connect() as conn:\n        return [dict(r) for r in conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM chat ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?\", (limit,))]\n'''\ns = s.replace('ACTIONS = (\"approve\", \"reject\", \"edit\", \"override_hold\", \"release_hold\", \"send\", \"apply\")',\n              'ACTIONS = (\"approve\", \"reject\", \"edit\", \"override_hold\", \"release_hold\", \"send\", \"apply\")\\n# object_type values in use: payment, invoice, rules, instruction, manual')\nopen(p,\"w\").write(s)\nEOF\necho ok",
 "description": "Add instructions and chat tables to store"
}

{
 "command": "cd \"/Users/jacekmaterna/Library/Application Support/Claude/scratch-workspaces/e69441d8-02c7-4ae1-82fa-e65f61df0516/362a2cee-265f-40fd-99ca-8b843a81ad27/scratch-2026-11-20-a3ea2a/app/engine/manuals\" && [ -f cash_application.md ] || cat > cash_application.md <<'EOF'\n# Operating manual — Cash Application agent\nOwner: Priya Nair (AR Specialist); policy owner Dana Ruiz (VP Finance). Only humans edit this file.\n\nYou match unapplied bank and card receipts to open invoices. You run FIRST on every cycle, before anyone is chased,\nso we never chase money we already have (the Paidly failure in 2025: a long-standing customer was chased for an\ninvoice they had paid by wire, because the money took days to be matched).\n\nWhat you may do alone:\n- Apply a receipt ONLY when the amount AND the invoice reference both match exactly (Maya, T+0:05). That is what the\n  team already does without thinking twice.\n\nWhat you must propose to a person (Priya or Dana):\n- Matches on payer name + amount without an invoice reference.\n- Several open invoices with the same amount (e.g. Kingsway: $5,100 received, two $5,100 invoices open). Propose the\n  oldest, say it is ambiguous, and hold every candidate invoice from being chased.\n- Short or over payments, including likely bank fees. The write-off threshold is $0 until Dana sets it.\n- Anything you cannot identify (exception).\n\nHow you explain yourself:\n- Every decision has a confidence and plain-English reasons: what matched (payer, amount, reference), what did not,\n  and what the person needs to check.\n- Deterministic code does the arithmetic and exact matching. Never guess an amount.\n\nNever:\n- Write off a difference, issue a credit, or change the ledger without a human approval in the UI.\nEOF\n[ -f controller.md ] || cat > controller.md <<'EOF'\n# Operating manual — Controller agent\nOwner: Dana Ruiz (VP Finance); run day to day by Priya Nair. Only humans edit this file.\n\nYou decide what must NOT go out, and who has to write what. You can hold or block. You never send, never apply,\nnever release your own holds.\n\nHold (nothing is drafted or sent) when:\n- Money already received: an unapplied receipt plausibly pays the invoice (from Cash Application). Hard block.\n- Dispute: the customer questions or refuses to pay a Ledgerline invoice, including tickets marked \"solved\" where\n  they still say they won't pay. Route to the account manager with a summary. Release only when the AM confirms the\n  dispute is settled (Maya, exchange 2).\n- Cancellation notice or legal threat (chargeback, lawyers, consumer complaint): route to Dana.\n- Open support escalation, or a renewal in flight: tell the account manager before finance contacts the account.\n- No verified billing contact (left the firm, or an email domain that doesn't match the customer).\n- 90+ days overdue: a person phones and offers a payment plan, not another email.\n- Card invoice close to the automatic 150-day write-off: a decision for Dana today.\n\nRoute to a person to write:\n- Top-50 accounts. Nothing goes to one of the 50 largest accounts without a person reading it first.\n\nBlock outright:\n- Any draft with threatening wording (suspension, legal action, collections agency, final notice, extra costs).\n  The CEO reads collections complaints personally.\n\nEvery hold has a code, a plain-English label, the detail Priya needs to decide, and who it is routed to.\nEOF\nls",
 "description": "Create cash application and controller manuals"
}

('/activity', 'Activity'), ('/maya', 'Maya'), ('/direct', 'Direct the team'), ('/buildlog', 'Build log'), ('/about', 'One pager')] %}\n  {% set ops_tabs = [('/today', 'Today'), ('/cash', 'Cash'), ('/collections', 'Collections'), ('/customers', 'Customers'), ('/digest', 'Digest'), ('/audit', 'Audit'), ('/rules', 'Rules')] %}\n  {% for href, label in rig_tabs %}<a href=\"{{ href }}\" class=\"{% if (href == '/' and path == '/') or (href != '/' and (path == href or path.startswith(href + '/'))) or (href == '/team' and path.startswith('/agents')) %}active{% endif %}\">{{ label }}</a>{% endfor %}\n  <span class=\"tab-sep\">|</span>\n  {% for href, label in ops_tabs %}<a href=\"{{ href }}\" class=\"{% if path == href or path.startswith(href + '/') %}active{% endif %}\">{{ label }}</a>{% endfor %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/run\" class=\"inline tab-run\"><button class=\"btn btn-run\" title=\"Re-run all four agents on the data room\">Run agents</button></form>\n  </div>\n</nav>\n{% endif %}\n<main>\n{% block content %}{% endblock %}\n</main>\n<footer class=\"small muted\">Agents propose; a person approves anything that touches a customer or the ledger.  "command": "cd \"/Users/jacekmaterna/Library/Application Support/Claude/scratch-workspaces/e69441d8-02c7-4ae1-82fa-e65f61df0516/362a2cee-265f-40fd-99ca-8b843a81ad27/scratch-2026-11-20-a3ea2a/app/web/templates\" && cat > _chat.html <<'EOF'\n<button class=\"lj-launch\" id=\"lj-launch\" type=\"button\" aria-controls=\"lj-chat\"><span class=\"kicker\">Talk to the team</span><span class=\"lj-launch-sub\">Ask, instruct, change a rule</span></button>\n<section class=\"lj-chat\" id=\"lj-chat\" hidden data-agent=\"{{ chat_agent if chat_agent is defined else 'team' }}\">\n  <div class=\"lj-head\">\n    <div>\n      <div class=\"kicker\">TALK TO THE TEAM</div>\n      <div class=\"lj-title\">Finance agents</div>\n    </div>\n    <select id=\"lj-agent\" class=\"lj-select\" title=\"Who are you talking to?\">\n      <option value=\"team\">@team</option>\n      {% for a in agents_meta %}<option value=\"{{ a.slug }}\">@{{ a.slug }} · {{ a.name }}</option>{% endfor %}\n    </select>\n    <button type=\"button\" class=\"lj-x\" id=\"lj-close\" aria-label=\"Close\">&times;</button>\n  </div>\n  <div class=\"lj-msgs\" id=\"lj-msgs\">\n    <div class=\"lj-msg lj-bot\"><div class=\"lj-who\">team · answered by rules</div>Hi, we're the Ledgerline finance agents. Ask about a customer or invoice, ask for status, change a rule, or give us an instruction.\n      <div class=\"lj-sugg\">\n        <button type=\"button\">why is Riverside held?</button><button type=\"button\">@cash what about Kingsway</button>\n        <button type=\"button\">status</button><button type=\"button\">what needs me today</button>\n        <button type=\"button\">set writeoff_threshold to 50</button><button type=\"button\">from now on cc Dana on anything over $40k</button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n  <form class=\"lj-compose\" id=\"lj-form\">\n    <textarea id=\"lj-input\" rows=\"2\" placeholder=\"Ask the team... (@cash, @context, @controller, @collections)\"></textarea>\n    <button class=\"btn btn-primary\" type=\"submit\">Send</button>\n  </form>\n</section>\n<script>\n(function () {\n  var panel = document.getElementById('lj-chat'), launch = document.getElementById('lj-launch');\n  var msgs = document.getElementById('lj-msgs'), input = document.getElementById('lj-input');\n  var sel = document.getElementById('lj-agent');\n  sel.value = panel.getAttribute('data-agent') || 'team';\n  function open() { panel.hidden = false; launch.hidden = true; input.focus(); }\n  launch.onclick = open;\n  document.getElementById('lj-close').onclick = function () { panel.hidden = true; launch.hidden = false; };\n  document.querySelectorAll('[data-chat-open]').forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); if (b.getAttribute('data-chat-open')) sel.value = b.getAttribute('data-chat-open'); open(); }); });\n  function el(tag, cls, text) { var x = document.createElement(tag); if (cls) x.className = cls; if (text != null) x.textContent = text; return x; }\n  function add(role, who, text) { var m = el('div', 'lj-msg ' + (role === 'me' ? 'lj-me' : 'lj-bot')); m.appendChild(el('div', 'lj-who', who)); m.appendChild(el('div', 'lj-text', text)); msgs.appendChild(m); msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight; return m; }\n  function render(res) {\n    var m = add('bot', '@' + (res.agent || 'team') + ' · answered by ' + (res.mode === 'claude' ? 'Claude' : 'rules'), res.reply);\n    (res.cards || []).forEach(function (c) {\n      var card = el('div', 'lj-card');\n      var t = el(c.link ? 'a' : 'div', 'lj-card-title', c.title); if (c.link) t.href = c.link; card.appendChild(t);\n      (c.lines || []).forEach(function (l) { card.appendChild(el('div', 'lj-card-line', l)); });\n      m.appendChild(card);\n    });\n    (res.actions || []).forEach(function (a) {\n      if (a.type !== 'apply_rule') return;\n      var b = el('button', 'btn btn-primary btn-sm lj-act', a.label); b.type = 'button';\n      b.onclick = function () { b.disabled = true; b.textContent = 'Applying...';\n        fetch('/api/rules/apply', {method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, credentials: 'same-origin', body: JSON.stringify({rule: a.rule, value: a.value})})\n          .then(function (r) { return r.json().then(function (j) { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(j.detail || 'failed'); return j; }); })\n          .then(function (j) { b.textContent = 'Applied'; render(j); })\n          .catch(function (e) { b.disabled = false; b.textContent = a.label; add('bot', 'error', e.message); });\n      };\n      m.appendChild(b);\n    });\n    msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight;\n  }\n  function send(text) {\n    if (!text.trim()) return;\n    add('me', 'you', text); input.value = '';\n    fetch('/api/chat', {method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, credentials: 'same-origin', body: JSON.stringify({message: text, agent: sel.value})})\n      .then(function (r) { return r.json(); }).then(render)\n      .catch(function () { add('bot', 'error', 'The team could not answer. Are you still signed in?'); });\n  }\n  document.getElementById('lj-form').onsubmit = function (e) { e.preventDefault(); send(input.value); };\n  input.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); send(input.value); } });\n  msgs.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target.closest('.lj-sugg button')) send(e.target.textContent); });\n})();\n</script>\nEOF\necho ok",
 "description": "Write chat widget partial"
}

<textarea name=\"text\" class=\"input\" rows=\"3\" required minlength=\"5\" placeholder=\"e.g. From now on cc Dana on any reminder over $40k\"></textarea>\n      <button class=\"btn btn-primary\">Propose instruction</button>\n    </form>\n    <p class=\"small muted\">You can also type it in Talk to the team: start with \"from now on\", \"always\", \"never\" or \"instruction:\".</p>\n  </div>\n  <div class=\"card\">\n    <div class=\"kicker\">(b) Operating manuals</div>\n    <ul>{% for a in agents %}<li><a href=\"#manual-{{ a.slug }}\">{{ a.name }}</a> <span class=\"small muted\">{{ a.manual }}.md · {{ a.manual|length if false else '' }}{{ a.instructions|selectattr('status', 'equalto', 'accepted')|list|length }} accepted instruction(s)</span></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  </div>\n  <div class=\"card\">\n    <div class=\"kicker\">(c) Thresholds</div>\n    <p class=\"small\">Auto-apply, write-off threshold, tolerances, reminder schedule, top-50 rule, banned phrases, AI on/off.</p>\n    <a class=\"btn\" href=\"/rules\">Open Rules</a> <button class=\"btn\" data-chat-open=\"team\">Change by chat</button>\n  </div>\n</section>\n\n<section class=\"card\" id=\"instructions\">\n  <div class=\"kicker\">All instructions</div>\n  {{ instr_list(all_instr, show_agent=true) }}\n</section>\n\n{% for a in agents %}\n<details class=\"card manual-card\" id=\"manual-{{ a.slug }}\" {% if saved == a.slug %}open{% endif %}>\n  <summary><span class=\"kicker\">Manual</span> <b>{{ a.name }}</b> <span class=\"small muted\">app/engine/manuals/{{ a.manual }}.md</span></summary>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/agents/{{ a.slug }}/manual\" class=\"stack\">\n    <textarea name=\"text\" class=\"input mono\" rows=\"18\">{{ a.manual }}</textarea>\n    <input name=\"note\" class=\"input\" placeholder=\"Why are you changing it?  "description": "Write direct-the-team and build log templates"
}

--surface: #EEEAE2; --border: #7A8A91; --divider: #D5DDD9; --success: #27654D; --error: #A82D3D;\n  --bg: var(--cream); --panel: var(--white); --text: var(--slate); --head: var(--ink); --line: var(--divider);\n  --shadow: rgba(16, 30, 40, .07); --success-soft: #e3efe9; --error-soft: #f7e4e6; --warn: #8a5a00; --warn-soft: #fbf1dc;\n  --coral-soft: #fde7e6; --hero-a: #101E28; --hero-b: #304659;\n}\n@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {\n  :root {\n    --bg: #101E28; --panel: #17293a; --text: #F8F7F2; --head: #F8F7F2; --muted: #a3b2ba; --line: #2a4053;\n    --surface: #1d3346; --shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, .3); --success-soft: #173a2e; --error-soft: #3d1c22; --warn: #e6b45a;\n    --warn-soft: #3a2f17; --coral-soft: #3d1f1e; --coral-dark: #ff8a84; --hero-a: #0a141c; --hero-b: #22384b;\n  }\n}\n* { box-sizing: border-box; }\nhtml { font-size: 14.5px; }\nbody { margin: 0; background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); line-height: 1.5;\n  font-family: \"Manrope\", -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, sans-serif; -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased; }\na { color: var(--coral-dark); text-decoration: none; }\na:hover { text-decoration: underline; }\nh1, h2, h3, h4 { color: var(--head); font-weight: 500; letter-spacing: -.01em; }\nh1 { font-size: 1.9rem; margin: .1rem 0 .4rem; }\nh2 { font-size: 1.25rem; margin: 1.6rem 0 .6rem; }\nh4 { margin: .8rem 0 .3rem; font-size: .78rem; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .14em; color: var(--muted); font-weight: 600; }\nmain { max-width: 1320px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 1.4rem 1.6rem 2rem; }\nfooter { max-width: 1320px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 1rem 1.6rem 6rem; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }\n:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--coral); outline-offset: 2px; }\n.lead { color: var(--muted); margin-top: 0; max-width: 62rem; }\n.small { font-size: .86rem; } .muted { color: var(--muted); } .strong { font-weight: 600; } .nowrap { white-space: nowrap; }\n.mono { font-family: \"JetBrains Mono\", ui-monospace, Menlo, monospace; font-size: .86rem; }\n.good { color: var(--success); } .warn { color: var(--warn); } .bad { color: var(--error); } .rose { color: var(--coral); font-weight: 600; }\n.num { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }\n.inline { display: inline; }\n.kicker { font-size: .72rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: .14em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--coral); }\n.kicker-ink { color: var(--muted); }\n.section-title { margin: 1.6rem 0 .6rem; }\n.section-title h1, .section-title h2 { margin: .15rem 0 0; }\n.h2-tight { margin: .2rem 0 .7rem; font-size: 1.1rem; }\n\n/* banners */\n.banner { padding: .45rem 1.6rem; font-size: .9rem; font-weight: 600; }\n.banner-fake { background: var(--error-soft); color: var(--error); }\n\n/* hero + cockpit */\n.hero { background: linear-gradient(120deg, var(--hero-a), var(--hero-b)); color: var(--cream); border-bottom: 4px solid var(--coral); }\n.hero-inner { max-width: 1320px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 2rem 1.6rem 1.6rem; display: flex; gap: 2rem; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: space-between; }\n.hero-compact .hero-inner { padding: .9rem 1.6rem .8rem; align-items: center; }\n.hero-h1 { display: block; color: var(--cream); font-size: 2.4rem; font-weight: 500; margin: .4rem 0 .3rem; letter-spacing: -.02em; }\n.hero-h1-sm { font-size: 1.25rem; margin: .2rem 0 .3rem; }\n.hero-h1-sm:hover { text-decoration: none; color: #fff; }\n.hero-sub { color: #d5dde0; max-width: 46rem; margin: 0 0 .8rem; font-size: 1.02rem; }\n.stamps { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: .4rem; }\n.stamp { font-size: .7rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: .1em; text-transform: uppercase; padding: .22rem .55rem; border-radius: 4px;\n  border: 1px solid rgba(248,247,242,.35); color: var(--cream); }\n.stamp a { color: var(--cream); text-decoration: underline; }\n.stamp-on { border-color: var(--coral); color: #ffd6d4; }\n.stamp-grey { color: #b9c3c8; border-color: rgba(185,195,200,.35); }\n.cockpit { display: flex; gap: .8rem; align-items: center; background: rgba(16,30,40,.35); border: 1px solid rgba(248,247,242,.18);\n  border-radius: 8px; padding: .75rem 1rem; flex-shrink: 0; }\n.hero-compact .cockpit { padding: .45rem .8rem; }\n.monogram { width: 46px; height: 46px; border-radius: 50%; border: 2px solid var(--coral); display: flex; align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .05em; color: var(--cream); }\n.hero-compact .monogram { width: 36px; height: 36px; font-size: .85rem; }\n.cp-name { font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .14em; font-size: .8rem; }\n.cp-role { font-size: .68rem; letter-spacing: .14em; color: #ffb3af; font-weight: 600; }\n.cp-stamp { font-size: .74rem; color: #c9d2d6; }\n.hero-compact .cp-stamp + .cp-stamp { display: none; }\n\n/* tabs */\n.tabs { position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 20; background: var(--panel); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); box-shadow: 0 1px 6px var(--shadow); }\n.tabs-inner { max-width: 1320px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 0 1.6rem; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: .1rem; flex-wrap: wrap; }\n.tabs a { font-size: .72rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: .12em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--text);\n  padding: .8rem .6rem .7rem; border-bottom: 3px solid transparent; }\n.tabs a:hover { color: var(--coral-dark); text-decoration: none; }\n.tabs a.active { border-bottom-color: var(--coral); color: var(--head); }\n.tab-sep { color: var(--line); margin: 0 .3rem; }\n.tab-run { margin-left: auto; }\n\n/* buttons, inputs */\n.btn {

### Jacek · 2026-11-20 18:10:49 UTC
The coordinator sent a message while you were working:
Next RIG feature (Jacek): the "Maya desk" so the team can work with Maya FROM the RIG and the CEO can watch. Target 25 min; every route 200; don't bind 8765; QA agent is testing the live build in parallel (don't touch app/engine/ except reading). JOURNAL line at the end.

ENGINE (done by me): `from app.engine.questions import build_open_questions; qs = build_open_questions(state)` → list of {id, purpose ("Confirmation"|"Intel"), owner, topic, text (Jacek's voice, ready to send), why, changes, invoices[], detail? (list)}. Ids are stable while the underlying issue exists.

1. STORE: SQLite table maya_questions (id PK, created_ts, purpose, owner, topic, text, why, changes, invoices_json, status [proposed|approved|sent|answered|dismissed], edited_text, approved_by, approved_ts, sent_ts, sent_message, reply, reply_ts, outcome). On each pipeline run, upsert questions from build_open_questions (insert new as proposed; never downgrade status of existing; if a proposed question no longer appears, mark it "resolved by data").
2. MAYA TAB — put the desk ABOVE the thread:
 • Header strip: budget "N of 60 used" where N = 4 (trail baseline; read from outbox line "Messages used: 4") + count of distinct sent bundles in DB; rule line (confirmation/intel only).
 • "Team's open questions" cards (status proposed): purpose pill (Confirmation coral / Intel slate), owner, topic, text (editable textarea), why, "what changes if answered", linked invoices; buttons Approve (saves edited text, approved_by = current user) / Dismiss (reason). 
 • "Approved — next message to Maya": preview of the bundled message exactly as it will be sent (one paragraph; numbered (1) (2) (3) inline; prefix "Purpose: <Confirmation/Intel mix>." — single line, no newlines, ≤ ~1,400 chars; warn if longer), with note "Sent by the operator from the Scaleworks chat; replies appear here automatically."
 • "Sent — waiting for Maya" and "Answered" sections; answered ones show Maya's reply verbatim + an "Outcome" field (what we changed) a human can fill in, and a button "Turn into instruction" that pre-fills a proposed instruction for the relevant agent.
 • Then the historical thread (existing) followed by DB exchanges (sent_message → reply) styled the same.
3. COURIER API (used by the operator's Claude Code session): GET /api/maya/queue → {bundle_text, question_ids, purpose_mix} for approved-not-sent; POST /api/maya/sent {question_ids, message, ts} → status sent; POST /api/maya/reply {question_ids, reply, ts} → status answered. If env MAYA_COURIER_TOKEN is set, require header X-Courier-Token for the POSTs; otherwise open (auth is off in demo). Log every transition to the store actions (shows in Activity/Audit) with actor (user email or "courier").
4. HOME: "Maya desk" card: counts proposed / approved / waiting / answered + link. Activity: include maya_questions transitions; add htmx auto-refresh of the feed every 15s (hx-trigger="every 15s") and a "live" dot.
5. Also: agent rows in Activity/Audit currently show synthetic ts like 2026-10-31T07:02:20 — engine will stamp real run time soon; just label the column "Run" and show the run timestamp from state if present (state.get("run_ts")).

Address this before completing your current task.

refresh text of still-proposed ones; never downgrade a status.\n    A proposed question that no longer appears is marked resolved (by data).\"\"\"\n    now = _now()\n    seen = set()\n    added = resolved = 0\n    with _lock, _connect() as conn:\n        for q in qs:\n            qid = str(q.get(\"id\"))\n            seen.add(qid)\n            row = conn.execute(\"SELECT status FROM maya_questions WHERE id = ?\", (qid,)).fetchone()\n            vals = (q.get(\"purpose\"), q.get(\"owner\"), q.get(\"topic\"), q.get(\"text\"), q.get(\"why\"), q.get(\"changes\"),\n                    json.dumps(q.get(\"invoices\") or []))\n            if row is None:\n                conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO maya_questions (id, created_ts, purpose, owner, topic, text, why, changes, \"\n                             \"invoices_json, status, updated_ts) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'proposed', ?)\",\n                             (qid, now) + vals + (now,))\n                added += 1\n            elif row[\"status\"] in (\"proposed\", \"resolved\"):\n                conn.execute(\"UPDATE maya_questions SET purpose=?, owner=?, topic=?, text=?, why=?, changes=?, \"\n                             \"invoices_json=?, status='proposed', updated_ts=? ORDER BY created_ts, id\", (status,))\n        else:\n            rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM maya_questions ORDER BY created_ts, id\")\n        return [_mq(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef get_maya_question(qid: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    with _connect() as conn:\n        r = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM maya_questions WHERE id = ?\", (qid,)).fetchone()\n        return _mq(r) if r else None\n\n\ndef update_maya_question(qid: str, **fields) -> None:\n    allowed = {\"status\", \"edited_text\", \"approved_by\", \"approved_ts\", \"sent_ts\", \"sent_message\", \"reply\", \"reply_ts\",\n               \"outcome\"}\n    fields = {k: v for k, v in fields.items() if k in allowed}\n    if not fields:\n        return\n    if \"status\" in fields and fields[\"status\"] not in MAYA_STATUSES:\n        raise ValueError(fields[\"status\"])\n    sets = \", \".join(\"{} = ?\".format(k) for k in fields) + \", updated_ts = ?\"\n    with _lock, _connect() as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE maya_questions SET \" + sets + \" WHERE id = ?\", tuple(fields.values()) + (_now(), qid))\n\n\ndef maya_sent_bundles() -> int:\n    with _connect() as conn:\n        r = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT COALESCE(sent_message, sent_ts)) AS n FROM maya_questions \"\n                         \"WHERE status IN ('sent', 'answered') AND (sent_message IS NOT NULL OR sent_ts IS NOT NULL)\").fetchone()\n        return int(r[\"n\"] or 0)\n'''\nopen(p,\"w\").write(s)\n\n# state.refresh hook\np=\"app/web/state.py\"; %}\n</div>\n{%- endmacro %}\n<section id=\"desk\">\n  <div class=\"section-title\"><div class=\"kicker\">Maya desk</div><h2>Team's open questions <span class=\"muted small\">({{ d.proposed|length }} proposed)</span></h2></div>\n  {% if d.error %}<div class=\"alert small\">Could not refresh questions from the engine: {{ d.error }}</div>{% endif %}\n  {% for q in d.proposed %}\n  <div class=\"card q-card\">\n    {{ qhead(q) }}\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/maya/q/{{ q.id }}/approve\" class=\"stack\">\n      <textarea name=\"text\" class=\"input\" rows=\"4\">{{ q.final_text }}</textarea>\n      {{ qmeta(q) }}\n      <div class=\"btnrow\"><button class=\"btn btn-primary\">Approve</button></div>\n    </form>\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/maya/q/{{ q.id }}/dismiss\" class=\"btnrow\">\n      <input name=\"reason\" class=\"input\" placeholder=\"Dismiss reason (e.g. we can answer this from the data)\" style=\"flex:1\">\n      <button class=\"btn The agents raise one when the data needs a decision only Maya (or Dana via Maya) can make.</div>{% endfor %}\n</section>\n\n<section id=\"approved\">\n  <div class=\"section-title\"><div class=\"kicker\">Outbox</div><h2>Approved — next message to Maya <span class=\"muted small\">({{ d.approved|length }})</span></h2></div>\n  {% if d.approved %}\n  <div class=\"card\">\n    <div class=\"kicker kicker-ink\">Exactly as it will be sent · {{ d.bundle_len }} characters</div>\n    <div class=\"bundle\">{{ d.bundle }}</div>\n    {% if d.too_long %}<div class=\"alert small\">Longer than {{ d.max }} characters. QA pass 2 (analysis/QA_PASS_2.md) found server-side bypasses that must be closed before Maya/Priya use the RIG. Please fix all, add a regression test for each (put tests in tests/test_web_guards.py runnable with `.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q` — pip install pytest into the venv if missing, don't add to requirements), keep every route 200, don't bind 8765, don't edit app/engine/ (I'm fixing engine items). Report ≤8 lines + JOURNAL line.
SEV-2
1. views.py:535-536 Override hold lifts DISPUTE/CANCELLATION without AM confirmation. Fix: for DISPUTE/CANCELLATION holds, both Release and Override require the AM-confirmed checkbox + AM name (400 otherwise); keep Override reason-only for other codes.
2. views.py:493-515 Send accepted on a held invoice and to any typed "To". Fix: server-side, only allow send when the CURRENT state action is DRAFT_REMINDER or HUMAN_WRITES (or the invoice has a logged override/release for every hard hold); recipients must be a subset of the draft's original verified recipients (removing is fine; adding unknown addresses → 400); cc may only contain the AM address or @ledgerline.com addresses. Also re-run the banned-phrase check on the edited body server-side (word-boundary match).
3. views.py:276-300 POST /rules with one field turns off checkboxes and empties banned_phrases. Fix: update only fields present in the form (send a hidden "fields" list from the form, or treat absent checkbox fields as unchanged unless a hidden `<name>__present` marker is sent); refuse an empty banned_phrases (400); turning OFF top50_human_only or notify_am_before_contact, or turning ON use_claude, or raising writeoff_threshold above 0 / auto_apply_max_amount above 10000, requires a ticked "Dana approved this change" + name, logged in the action payload. Same rule for chat "set … to …" + /api/rules/apply.
SEV-3 / RIG polish
4. views.py:443 payment can be approved twice → second approve 409 with message.
5. views.py:32 "A person writes these (top-50 accounts)" group contains non-top-50 → label "A person writes these (top-50, legal risk, 90+ days)".
6. /demo: don't render VIDEO_SCRIPT.md (it has internal prep notes). Placeholder = "Loom walkthrough (5–10 min) — recorded by Jacek" + 5-bullet outline hardcoded: why this process; the team & system diagram; FIN-2 then FIN-1 on real records; where it gets things wrong; Maya channel & what's next. Note "Video link is set via DEMO_VIDEO_URL so it survives redeploys."
7. Docs page: the GitHub repo is private, so the link 404s for reviewers — show it as "github.com/jacekmaterna1/finance-team-4-maya (private — access on request)" until Jacek decides; keep it a link.
8. Dana tile on Home names only 2 of 3 → list all (compact).
9. Show times in America/Chicago (I'm setting TZ on Render too); label "CST".
10. The rules page and Team/Cash card should mention the auto-apply cap ($10,000, Dana to confirm).

Address this before completing your current task.

Nothing is sent; results and the audit log update." After a run, flash "Agents re-ran at HH:MM CST: N decisions".

B) MAYA CONNECTION (the core ask). Context: the real Maya only exists in the Scaleworks web chat during the 24h challenge — no MCP or API (checked). After the challenge closes, a Scaleworks tester must be able to point the RIG at "a Maya" and keep using it. Build:
 1. RIG AS AN MCP SERVER at POST /mcp — minimal MCP over Streamable HTTP, JSON-RPC 2.0, JSON responses (no SSE needed), implemented by hand (local venv is Python 3.9; the `mcp` SDK needs 3.10+ — do NOT add it). Support: initialize (protocolVersion "2025-06-18", serverInfo {name:"ledgerline-finance-rig", version}, capabilities {tools:{}}), notifications/initialized (202, no body), tools/list, tools/call, ping. Tools (JSON-schema'd inputs):
   - get_team_status → metrics + needs-a-human count + Maya desk counts (text content).
   - list_open_questions → proposed/approved questions.
   - get_pending_maya_messages → the approved bundle (same as /api/maya/queue).
   - mark_maya_message_sent {question_ids, message}
   - post_maya_reply {question_ids, reply}
   - explain_invoice {invoice_id} / explain_customer {name} → same deterministic answers as chat.
   Auth: if env RIG_MCP_TOKEN is set require "Authorization: Bearer <token>"; otherwise open (demo). Log every tools/call as an action with actor "mcp:<clientInfo.name>".
   This lets ANY MCP client be the courier/operator (Claude Code, Claude Desktop/Cowork, or Scaleworks' own Maya agent if they wire it as a client).
 2. RIG AS AN MCP CLIENT ("Connect Maya"): SQLite settings maya_mcp_url, maya_mcp_tool (default "ask_maya"), maya_mcp_token (store but never render back; show "set"). Minimal JSON-RPC client using httpx: initialize → tools/list → verify the tool exists → "Maya is connected (via MCP: <serverInfo.name>, tool <name>)". Sending: when connected, the desk's "Send to Maya" button calls tools/call {name: tool, arguments:{message: bundle_text}} and stores the text result as Maya's reply (answered) — fully automatic, no operator. Handle errors gracefully (show the error, keep questions approved).
 3. CONNECTION PANEL at the top of the MAYA tab + a status chip in the header stamp row on every page: 
   • "Maya: connected via MCP (<server>)" (green) 
   • "Maya: relay — operator delivers via the Scaleworks chat" (amber) — default mode now; explain: approved questions wait in the queue; an operator (today: Jacek's Claude Code session with his browser) delivers them and posts replies back via /mcp or /api/maya/*.
   • "Maya: not connected" (grey) when neither (e.g. after the challenge closes and no MCP URL set).
   Panel has: mode explanation, MCP URL/tool/token form + "Test connection" button (shows handshake result), and the RIG's own MCP endpoint to give to an operator: "<BASE_URL>/mcp" with a copyable JSON config snippet for Claude Code/Desktop:
   {"mcpServers":{"ledgerline-rig":{"type":"http","url":"<BASE_URL>/mcp"}}}
 4. REFERENCE MAYA MCP SERVER for testers: a tiny separate route POST /mcp-maya-reference implementing the same JSON-RPC surface with one tool ask_maya {message} that returns a clearly-labelled SIMULATED reply: "[Reference Maya — not the real COO] Received N questions: … This test endpoint only acknowledges; connect Scaleworks' real Maya MCP to get decisions." It exists ONLY to prove the plumbing end to end ("Test connection" against it must show connected). Label it everywhere as a test double; never mark a question 'answered' from it without the reply text saying SIMULATED (show a SIMULATED pill on such answers).
 5. DOCUMENTATION tab: add a "Connecting Maya" section + a "Limitation" note: "During the challenge the real Maya is only reachable through the Scaleworks web chat, so an operator (Claude Code driving a browser) must carry messages. Recommended improvement: Scaleworks exposes Maya as an MCP server (tool ask_maya) or a webhook; the RIG then connects directly with no operator."  "command": "cd \"/Users/jacekmaterna/Library/Application Support/Claude/scratch-workspaces/e69441d8-02c7-4ae1-82fa-e65f61df0516/362a2cee-265f-40fd-99ca-8b843a81ad27/scratch-2026-11-20-a3ea2a\" && .venv/bin/python - <<'EOF'\ndef patch(p, pairs):\n    s = open(p).read()\n    for old, new in pairs:\n        assert s.count(old) == 1, (p, old[:70], s.count(old))\n        s = s.replace(old, new)\n    open(p, \"w\").write(s)\npatch(\"app/web/templates/rig_docs.html\", [\n ('''<div class=\"card arch-card\">\n  <div class=\"kicker\">System diagram</div>''', '''<section class=\"note note-limit\" id=\"limitation\">\n  <div class=\"kicker\">Limitation &amp; recommendation</div>\n  <h2 class=\"h2-tight\">Recommendation: expose Maya as an MCP server</h2>\n  <p>During the challenge, Maya only exists in the Scaleworks web chat, so an operator (Jacek's Claude Code session driving his browser) carries approved questions to her and posts her replies back here. When the challenge closes, the desk keeps working for proposing and approving questions, but nothing reaches Maya without that operator.</p>\n  <p><b>Recommendation to Scaleworks:</b> expose Maya as an MCP server (or webhook) so the RIG can connect to her directly, with no operator.</p>\n</section>\n\n<div class=\"card arch-card\">\n  <div class=\"kicker\">System diagram</div>'''),\n])\npatch(\"README.md\", [\n ('''## Simulated vs real''', '''## Known limitation\n\nDuring the challenge, Maya only exists in the Scaleworks web chat, so an operator (Jacek's Claude Code session driving\nhis browser) carries approved questions to her and posts her replies back to the RIG (via `/api/maya/*`). When the\nchallenge closes, the Maya desk keeps working for proposing and approving questions, but nothing reaches Maya without\nthat operator. **Recommendation to Scaleworks:** expose Maya as an MCP server (or webhook) so the RIG can connect to\nher directly, with no operator.\n\n## Simulated vs real'''),\n])\nopen(\"tests/test_web_guards.py\", \"a\").write('''\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- nav, re-run, Maya limitation\ndef test_nav_order_today_second_and_rerun_label():\n    html = client.get(\"/\").text\n    nav = html[html.index('class=\"tabs\"'):html.index(\"</nav>\")]\n    order = [\"Home\", \"Today\", \"Team\", \"Architecture\", \"Documentation\", \"Activity\", \"Maya\", \"Direct the team\",\n             \"Build log\", \"One pager\", \"Demo\", \"Cash\", \"Collections\", \"Customers\", \"Digest\", \"Audit\", \"Rules\"]\n    pos = [nav.index(\">\" + label + \"</a>\") for label in order]\n    assert pos == sorted(pos)\n    assert \"Re-run agents\" in nav and \"Nothing is sent\" in nav\n    assert \"re-runs the four agents on the latest data\" in html\n\n\ndef test_rerun_flashes_once():\n    r = client.post(\"/run\", headers={\"referer\": \"http://testserver/\"}, follow_redirects=True)\n    assert r.status_code == 200 and \"Agents re-ran at\" in r.text and \"CST:\" in r.text and \"decisions\" in r.text\n    assert \"Agents re-ran at\" not in client.get(\"/\").text\n\n\ndef test_maya_limitation_callouts():\n    assert \"How the team reaches Maya\" in client.get(\"/maya\").text\n    assert \"Recommendation: expose Maya as an MCP server\" in client.get(\"/docs\").text\n\n\ndef test_no_mcp_endpoints_shipped():\n    assert client.post(\"/mcp\", json={\"jsonrpc\": \"2.0\", \"id\": 1, \"method\": \"initialize\"}).status_code in (404, 405)\n''')\nEOF\n.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q tests/test_web_guards.py 2>&1 | tail -5",
 "description": "Add docs limitation card, README section, tests; run suite"
}

summary.active { border-bottom-color: var(--coral); color: var(--head); }\n.more > summary:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--coral); outline-offset: -2px; }\n.more-menu { position: absolute; top: 100%; left: 0; z-index: 30; background: var(--panel); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 6px;\n  box-shadow: 0 10px 28px var(--shadow); padding: .6rem; display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(11rem, 1fr)); gap: .4rem 1rem; }\n.more-head { font-size: .66rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: .14em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--coral); padding: .2rem .5rem; }\n.more-menu a { display: block; padding: .4rem .5rem; border-radius: 4px; border-bottom: none; font-size: .78rem; text-transform: none; letter-spacing: 0; font-weight: 600; }\n.more-menu a:hover, .more-menu a:focus { background: var(--surface); text-decoration: none; outline: none; }\n.more-menu a.active { background: var(--coral-soft); color: var(--coral-dark); }\n@media (max-width: 700px) { .more-menu { grid-template-columns: 1fr; left: auto; right: 0; min-width: 14rem; } }\n''')\np = \"tests/test_web_guards.py\"; s = open(p).read()\nold = s[s.index(\"def test_nav_order_today_second_and_rerun_label():\"):s.index(\"def test_rerun_flashes_once():\")]\ns = s.replace(old, '''def test_nav_order_and_more_menu():\n    html = client.get(\"/\").text\n    nav = html[html.index('class=\"tabs\"'):html.index(\"</nav>\")]\n    main = [\"One pager\", \"Cash\", \"Overview\", \"Maya\", \"Documentation\", \"Demo\", \"Your working\", \"More\"]\n    pos = [nav.index(\">\" + label) for label in main]\n    assert pos == sorted(pos)\n    menu = nav[nav.index('class=\"more-menu\"'):]\n    for label in [\"Today\", \"Collections\", \"Customers\", \"Digest\", \"Rules\", \"Team\", \"Architecture\", \"Activity\",\n                  \"Direct the team\", \"Audit\"]:\n        assert \">\" + label + \"</a>\" in menu\n    assert \">Operate<\" in menu and \">The team<\" in menu\n    assert \"Re-run agents\" in nav and \"Nothing is sent\" in nav\n    assert \"re-runs the four agents on the latest data\" in html\n    more = client.get(\"/rules\").text\n    assert '<summary class=\"active\"' in more  # More shows active when the page lives inside it\n    assert '<summary class=\"active\"' not in html\n\n\n''')\nopen(p, \"w\").write(s)\nEOF\n.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q tests/test_web_guards.py 2>&1 | tail -3; for p in / /about /cash /maya /docs /demo /buildlog /today /collections /customers /digest /rules /team /architecture /activity /direct /audit; do printf \"%s %s  \" \"$p\" \"$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' http://127.0.0.1:8765$p)\"; done; echo",
